# Flow diagram — FIN v2 (مخطط التنقل — الحسابات العامة)

One flow block per navigation path. Screens carry the atoms (`SCR-*`); flows carry none of
their own, per `profile.conventions.composite_screen`.

Delta v2 (CS-FIN-001): this file carries no id definition, so it is restated in full as current
state. The v1 flows are unchanged except as noted here. One of them, "Engine rules", has its sequence narrowed by the
decision in ADR-FIN-062: lines are added one per submit. The six v2 flows are marked `v2`.
The frontend build's consumer feedback is answered by ADR-FIN-065: the five screens that own a child collection (dimensions,
engine rules, templates, allocation rules, fiscal years) navigate list → `/new` | `/:id` record page. The two
statement drill-downs carry the group's account type only.

```
FLOW — Chart of accounts / شجرة الحسابات                traces=US-FIN-001,REQ-FIN-001,SCR-FIN-001
Screens   : SCR-FIN-001
Sequence  : FIN → Setup → Chart of accounts → Search → Entry (create/update) → Deactivate
Trigger   : finance administrator manages the account hierarchy
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Dimensions & values / تعريف الأبعاد وقيمها       traces=US-FIN-002,REQ-FIN-004,SCR-FIN-002
Screens   : SCR-FIN-002
Sequence  : FIN → Setup → Dimensions (list, /fin/dimensions) → New (/fin/dimensions/new, header saved alone) | open a row → record page (/fin/dimensions/:dimensionId: header + values beneath) → Value entry (Drawer, ?valueAction=create) → Deactivate value
Trigger   : finance administrator defines a new analysis segment
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Engine rules / قواعد المحرك                       traces=US-FIN-003,REQ-FIN-007,SCR-FIN-003
Screens   : SCR-FIN-003
Sequence  : FIN → Setup → Engine rules (list, /fin/rules) → New (/fin/rules/new, rule saved alone) | open a row → record page (/fin/rules/:ruleId: rule header + lines beneath) → Line entry (Drawer, ?lineAction=create — one line per submit, ADR-FIN-062) → Deactivate rule
Trigger   : finance administrator maps an event type to posting lines, as data
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Recurring/reversing templates / قوالب متكررة/عكسية traces=US-FIN-006,REQ-FIN-022,SCR-FIN-004
Screens   : SCR-FIN-004
Sequence  : FIN → Setup → Recurring/reversing templates (list, /fin/recurring-templates) → New (/fin/recurring-templates/new: header + lines composed in the Line entry Drawer, ?lineAction=create, created in one call) | open a row → record page (/fin/recurring-templates/:templateId) → Run / Deactivate
Trigger   : accountant sets up an accrual/reversal that recurs on a schedule
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Allocation rules / قواعد التوزيع                   traces=US-FIN-007,REQ-FIN-025,SCR-FIN-005
Screens   : SCR-FIN-005
Sequence  : FIN → Setup → Allocation rules (list, /fin/allocation-rules) → New (/fin/allocation-rules/new: header + targets composed in the Target entry Drawer, ?targetAction=create, created in one call) | open a row → record page (/fin/allocation-rules/:allocationRuleId) → Run / Deactivate
Trigger   : accountant defines how a source balance splits across targets
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Manual journal entry / إدخال يدوي                  traces=US-FIN-005,REQ-FIN-014,SCR-FIN-006
Screens   : SCR-FIN-006
Sequence  : FIN → Operations → Journal entries → Search → Entry (header + lines) → Post
Trigger   : accountant records an adjustment or opening entry with no source event
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Review and reverse a posted entry / عكس قيد مُرحَّل traces=US-FIN-009,REQ-FIN-028,SCR-FIN-006
Screens   : SCR-FIN-006
Sequence  : FIN → Operations → Journal entries → Search → open a POSTED row → Reverse
Trigger   : accountant corrects a mistake without ever editing the original
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Fiscal years & periods / السنوات والفترات المالية   traces=US-FIN-010,REQ-FIN-031,SCR-FIN-007
Screens   : SCR-FIN-007
Sequence  : FIN → Control → Fiscal periods & years (list, /fin/periods) → New (/fin/periods/new: create year, generates periods) → record page (/fin/periods/:fiscalYearId) → Open/Soft-close per period
Trigger   : finance administrator sets up the accounting calendar
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Approve period close / اعتماد إغلاق الفترة           traces=US-FIN-011,REQ-FIN-037,SCR-FIN-007
Screens   : SCR-FIN-007
Sequence  : FIN → Control → Fiscal periods & years → year record page (/fin/periods/:fiscalYearId) → Soft Closed period → Hard-close (approval; the server's FIN-403-FORBIDDEN enforces the separation of duty — ADR-FIN-065)
Trigger   : financial controller, independent of any entry creator, closes the books for a period
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Run year-end close / تشغيل إقفال نهاية السنة          traces=US-FIN-010,REQ-FIN-036,SCR-FIN-007
Screens   : SCR-FIN-007
Sequence  : FIN → Control → Fiscal periods & years → year record page (/fin/periods/:fiscalYearId, all periods Hard Closed) → Run year-end close
Trigger   : approver rolls the ledger into the next fiscal year
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Account ledger / دفتر الحساب                           traces=US-FIN-012,REQ-FIN-039,SCR-FIN-008
Screens   : SCR-FIN-008
Sequence  : FIN → Reports → Account ledger → select account + range → running-balance list
Trigger   : accountant/controller inspects one account's live-derived history
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Trial balance / ميزان المراجعة                          traces=US-FIN-013,REQ-FIN-040,SCR-FIN-009
Screens   : SCR-FIN-009
Sequence  : FIN → Reports → Trial balance → select period → balanced account list
Trigger   : financial controller reviews the always-balanced control report
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Balance sheet / الميزانية العمومية                        traces=US-FIN-014,REQ-FIN-041,SCR-FIN-010
Screens   : SCR-FIN-010
Sequence  : FIN → Reports → Balance sheet → select fiscal year (+ as-of date) → grouped statement
Trigger   : financial controller reviews year-over-year balance-sheet continuity
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Income statement / قائمة الدخل                            traces=US-FIN-015,REQ-FIN-042,SCR-FIN-011
Screens   : SCR-FIN-011
Sequence  : FIN → Reports → Income statement → select fiscal year (+ period range) → grouped statement
Trigger   : financial controller reviews period results that open at zero each year
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Dimension reports / تقارير الأبعاد                          traces=US-FIN-016,REQ-FIN-043,SCR-FIN-012
Screens   : SCR-FIN-012
Sequence  : FIN → Reports → Dimension reports → select dimension (+ value, period) → account×value rows
Trigger   : financial controller analyzes results per project/department/investor
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Drill-down: ledger to source entry                          traces=US-FIN-019,REQ-FIN-046,SCR-FIN-008,SCR-FIN-006
Screens   : SCR-FIN-008, SCR-FIN-006
Sequence  : Account ledger row → originating JournalEntry (SCR-FIN-006, read-only) → its eventReference
Trigger   : controller explains a ledger row back to its root cause
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Drill-down: trial balance to ledger                          traces=US-FIN-019,REQ-FIN-046,SCR-FIN-009,SCR-FIN-008
Screens   : SCR-FIN-009, SCR-FIN-008
Sequence  : Trial balance row (accountId) → Account ledger (SCR-FIN-008), same period
Trigger   : controller drills a control-report row into its account detail
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Drill-down: balance sheet to trial balance                    traces=US-FIN-019,REQ-FIN-046,SCR-FIN-010,SCR-FIN-009
Screens   : SCR-FIN-010, SCR-FIN-009
Sequence  : Balance sheet group → Trial balance (SCR-FIN-009) at /fin/reports/trial-balance?accountTypeCode=<the group's accountTypeCode> — the trial balance publishes only periodId and accountTypeCode, and the balance sheet returns no period, so no account and no period is carried (ADR-FIN-065)
Trigger   : controller drills a statement line into the control report behind it
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Drill-down: income statement to trial balance                  traces=US-FIN-019,REQ-FIN-046,SCR-FIN-011,SCR-FIN-009
Screens   : SCR-FIN-011, SCR-FIN-009
Sequence  : Income statement group → Trial balance (SCR-FIN-009) at /fin/reports/trial-balance?accountTypeCode=<the group's accountTypeCode> — the trial balance publishes no account parameter and takes one period, not a range, so no account and no period is carried (ADR-FIN-065)
Trigger   : controller drills a statement line into the control report behind it
Priority  : MEDIUM
```

```
FLOW — Account mappings / روابط الحسابات  (v2)                    traces=US-FIN-025,REQ-FIN-054,REQ-FIN-055,REQ-FIN-056,REQ-FIN-057,SCR-FIN-013
Screens   : SCR-FIN-013
Sequence  : FIN → Setup → Account mappings → Search (event type, business field, business value, account, active) → Entry: new mapping (Save) | existing mapping: change account (Save) → Deactivate (confirmed row action)
Trigger   : finance administrator adds, re-points or retires the account a business value resolves to, as data and never in code
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Engine rule line derived by mapping, with dimension tags  (v2)   traces=US-FIN-021,US-FIN-022,US-FIN-026,REQ-FIN-049,REQ-FIN-062,SCR-FIN-003
Screens   : SCR-FIN-003
Sequence  : Engine rules → rule record page (/fin/rules/:ruleId) → lines → Line entry (?lineAction=create) → derivation MAPPING + business field (or CONSTANT + account code) → Dimension tags (second level: per dimension, a constant value or a business field) → Save line (one submit, line and tags together)
Trigger   : finance administrator makes an event-built line choose cash or bank by payment method, or the expense account by expense type, and stamp organisation and branch on it
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Rule line to the mappings it resolves through  (v2)         traces=US-FIN-021,US-FIN-024,REQ-FIN-050,REQ-FIN-053,SCR-FIN-003,SCR-FIN-013
Screens   : SCR-FIN-003, SCR-FIN-013
Sequence  : Engine rules → a MAPPING line → "mappings" link → Account mappings (SCR-FIN-013), filtered by the rule's event type and the line's business field → add the missing value's mapping
Trigger   : finance administrator checks, before the migration data is loaded, that every business value a line will meet has an active mapping, and completes an unmapped one instead of letting events be rejected
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Mapping to its account  (v2)                                traces=US-FIN-023,REQ-FIN-060,SCR-FIN-013,SCR-FIN-001
Screens   : SCR-FIN-013, SCR-FIN-001
Sequence  : Account mappings row → mapped account cell → Chart of accounts (SCR-FIN-001), filtered by that account's code
Trigger   : finance administrator confirms that the mapped account is still an active leaf that accepts direct posting
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Review an event-built entry's attribution  (v2)             traces=US-FIN-029,US-FIN-026,REQ-FIN-070,SCR-FIN-006
Screens   : SCR-FIN-006
Sequence  : FIN → Operations → Journal entries → Search (journal type EVENT_GENERATED) → open the entry (its eventReference shown in the header) → lines with their accounts and dimension values, shown exactly as a manual line's
Trigger   : financial controller checks, during the parallel run, that an event-built entry carries organisation and branch like the legacy entry it mirrors
Priority  : HIGH
```

```
FLOW — Reverse an event-built entry  (v2)                          traces=US-FIN-032,REQ-FIN-077,SCR-FIN-006
Screens   : SCR-FIN-006
Sequence  : Journal entries → open a POSTED event-built entry → Reverse → the reversal carries the same accounts, amounts and dimension values with the opposite direction
Trigger   : accountant corrects an event-built entry through the same reversal-only path as any other
Priority  : —
```

## Reconciliation self-check (SRS B1–B4 ↔ draft) — no human gate

```
RECONCILIATION — FIN v2
B1 every US-* used in a flow has an SRS counterpart (REQ/AC/screen)   → v1: US-FIN-001..019 as before · v2: US-FIN-021, 022, 023, 024, 025, 026, 029, 032 used, each with a REQ and a SCR-REQ in the SRS matrix; none invented
   not on a flow: US-FIN-020, 027, 028, 030 and 031 are event-path behaviours (build, validate, reject, answer the consumer) with no navigation of their own: they are bound to the event-build endpoint in the plan, which no screen invokes (ADR-FIN-007), and they appear on no flow. US-FIN-033 is DEFERRED and has no REQ
B2 no RULE-* contradicts a flow/spec outcome                          → no contradiction. RULE-FIN-018 (DIRECT gone): the derivation list is runtime-loaded and offers what MDL holds. RULE-FIN-022/023/024: pre-empted on SCR-FIN-013's form, server-authoritative. RULE-FIN-025/026: pre-empted on the tag editor
B3 every field/permission on a screen exists in the SRS               → checked against SRS A3 (ENT-FIN-010, 015, 016 re-emitted in v2), SCR-REQ-FIN-003/006/013 §B3/§B4 and the Access summary. None extra, none missing
B4 every screen entry of the SRS has exactly one SCR-* block          → 13 SCR-REQ-FIN-* → 13 SCR-FIN-* (1:1): SCR-REQ-FIN-013 → SCR-FIN-013 added, composite rule applied (Search + Entry = one screen)
RESULT  reconciled 13 · reworked 3 (SCR-FIN-003, SCR-FIN-006, SCR-FIN-013 — the v2 screen requirements) · ADRs ADR-FIN-062 (rule-line composition). ADR-FIN-064 (the published v2 surface, superseding ADR-FIN-060) and ADR-FIN-061 bind the plan, not the design
```

<<<INPUT: ui-ux-spec (baseline — continue it, never restart it)>>>
# UI/UX spec — FIN v2 (مواصفات الواجهة — الحسابات العامة)

Fields, permissions and states below are copied from the SRS (`srs-fin.md`, folded v2 state) with no
addition and no omission. They are narrowed only where an ADR records a divergence between the SRS's
B1/B3 wording and the published surface. Cross-module display dependencies (`UXD-*`) are defined once
here (§ Cross-module display dependencies) and cited by screen, never restated.

Delta v2 (CS-FIN-001):
- **Added:** SCR-FIN-013 (account mappings), and UXD-FIN-013 … UXD-FIN-015 for the v2 lookup keys a screen now displays.
- **Modified:**
  - SCR-FIN-003 and SCR-FIN-006, whose screen requirements v2 modified.
  - Every other screen block, which now carries the `Composition` line this engine requires (§A.4). Their fields,
    permissions and states are unchanged, apart from the endpoint each one cites: it is named by the contract id
    the republished api-docs carry (ADR-FIN-064), and by verb and path only for the fiscal-year search, which
    carries none (ADR-FIN-061).
  - UXD-FIN-007, which SCR-FIN-013 now also displays.
  - Consumer feedback from the frontend build (ADR-FIN-065). SCR-FIN-002, 003, 004, 005 and 007 own a child
    collection, so each becomes a list page plus a `/new` and a `/:id` record page. SCR-FIN-006 gains the
    fiscal-year select binding. SCR-FIN-010 gains the drill-down carrier. SCR-FIN-011 gains the independent period
    bounds. Every option select discloses a capped set, and no affordance is hidden on the caller's
    action-level permissions.
- **Unchanged:** every other UXD.

SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE — Where an Entry sub-view carries a repeating child collection or a
picker over an unbounded entity set, that collection or picker does NOT render inline in the
Entry Drawer. The Entry shows a summary row (label · edit trigger · capped preview) and the
collection/picker opens as a second-level Drawer which RETURNS A VALUE and never saves.
Exactly one save, in the Entry's own footer. No second scroll container inside a Drawer body.
The second-level panel's open state is a URL param of its own (`?picker=…`), so Back closes it,
Escape closes only it, and it deep-links. The panel renders as a sibling of the first level,
never inside its element. Each screen block cites this clause in its `Composition` line.
Full rule: `ui-secondary-detail-panels` → `erp-drawer-zustand` §10.

## SCR-FIN-001 — شجرة الحسابات / Chart of accounts
Traces            : REQ-FIN-001, REQ-FIN-002, REQ-FIN-003, AC-FIN-001, UXD-FIN-001, UXD-FIN-002
UI pattern        : Search + Entry, ONE screen (composite) — true hierarchy (parent/child); no by-id read, entry hydrates from the search row (ADR-FIN-006)
Sub-views         : Search · Entry
Fields shown      : code, nameAr, nameEn, accountTypeCode (UXD-FIN-001), natureCode (UXD-FIN-002), parentAccountId, isLeafFl, isActiveFl, isRetainedEarningsFl (read-only) — labels ar/en per field, per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-001
Composition       : parentAccountId → summary row + second level (the selected parent is a summary row in the Entry; the account-tree picker is a second-level Drawer that returns the chosen parent and never saves — SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE; the tree never renders inline) · submits: one — Save in the Entry footer (create or update); Deactivate is a separate confirmed action, not a second save
Permissions       : FIN_ACCOUNTS — VIEW, CREATE, UPDATE (covers deactivate) — reference only, names owned by SEC/backend registry
Cross-module data : accountTypeCode → UXD-FIN-001 · natureCode → UXD-FIN-002 | none else
Navigation        : v2 — reached from SCR-FIN-013's mapped-account cell, landing with the search filtered by that account's code
States            : empty · loading · error (generic, catalog code FIN-404-ACCOUNT / FIN-409-ACCOUNT-DUP / FIN-409-PARENT-NOT-LEAF-ELIGIBLE / FIN-409-HAS-CHILDREN)

## SCR-FIN-002 — تعريف الأبعاد وقيمها / Dimension definition & values
Traces            : REQ-FIN-004, REQ-FIN-005, REQ-FIN-006, AC-FIN-004
UI pattern        : master-detail RECORD (ADR-FIN-065 — erp-drawer-zustand §11 / ui-master-detail-records): a dimension OWNS its values, so the screen is a list page + a record page, not two panes on one route; parent dimension has no deactivate (SRS §B4, deliberate); no by-id endpoint — the record page hydrates on a cold deep link through the published dimension search filtered by `dimensionPk EQUALS id` (ADR-FIN-006 as narrowed by ADR-FIN-065)
Sub-views         : List (dimensions, whole viewport) · Record page (header form inline in a collapsible section; the values collection beneath it) · New (header form; values locked with the reason stated — a value is created against the dimension's id) · Value entry (a Drawer on its own search param, over the record page)
Fields shown      : dimension: code, nameAr, nameEn · value: code, nameAr, nameEn, sortOrder, isActiveFl — per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-002/003
Composition       : none — the dimension header (three fields) and the value form (four fields) each create one record with no secondary detail; the values collection is inline beneath the header on the record page, scrolling with the page body · submits: one per form — Save dimension (the header section's own footer), Save value (the Value entry Drawer); value deactivate is a separate confirmed row action
Permissions       : FIN_DIMENSIONS — VIEW, CREATE, UPDATE (dimension-value deactivate only — API-FIN-035) — reference only; every affordance renders for a holder of the screen and the server's FIN-403-FORBIDDEN decides (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065)
Cross-module data : none
Navigation        : list row → `/fin/dimensions/:dimensionId` · New → `/fin/dimensions/new`, and after the save the page moves to the record page using the id of the create response · `?valueAction=create` opens the Value entry · an id the published search does not return renders a "not found" notice with a way back to the list, never an empty form
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-409-DIMENSION-DUP / FIN-409-DIMVALUE-DUP / FIN-404-DIMVALUE) · not found (record page)

## SCR-FIN-003 — قواعد المحرك / Engine rules
Traces            : REQ-FIN-007, REQ-FIN-008, REQ-FIN-009, REQ-FIN-048, REQ-FIN-049, REQ-FIN-062, REQ-FIN-064, REQ-FIN-065, AC-FIN-007, AC-FIN-048, AC-FIN-049, AC-FIN-066, AC-FIN-069, AC-FIN-070, UXD-FIN-013, UXD-FIN-014
UI pattern        : master-detail RECORD (ADR-FIN-065 — erp-drawer-zustand §11 / ui-master-detail-records): a rule OWNS its lines (SRS SCR-REQ-FIN-003 B1, MODIFIED v2), so the screen is a list page + a record page, not two panes on one route; no rule update, no by-id endpoint, no line update and no line delete (SRS §B4, ADR-FIN-006) — the record page hydrates on a cold deep link through the published rule search filtered by `eventTypeRulePk EQUALS id` (ADR-FIN-065); a line and its tags are one submission and share the line's lifecycle (ADR-FIN-025); lines are added one per submit on the record page, never batched behind the rule's save (ADR-FIN-062)
Sub-views         : List (rules, whole viewport) · New (the rule header, saved alone; lines locked with the reason stated — a line is added against the rule's id) · Record page (the rule header read-only in a collapsible section — no rule update is published — with its lines beneath) · Line entry (a Drawer on its own search param over the record page: add one line, with its tags)
Fields shown      : rule: eventTypeCode (UXD-FIN-007), nameAr, nameEn, isActiveFl · line: lineNo (read-only), accountDerivationTypeCode (UXD-FIN-008 — CONSTANT / MAPPING; DIRECT is no longer offered, RULE-FIN-018), accountDerivationValue (CONSTANT only — the constant account code), accountBusinessFieldCode (MAPPING only, UXD-FIN-013), amountSourceTypeCode (UXD-FIN-009), amountSourceValue, directionCode (UXD-FIN-002), distributionTypeCode (UXD-FIN-010), isRemainderFl · dimension tag, repeating per line: dimensionId, valueSourceCode (UXD-FIN-014), dimensionValueId (CONSTANT only), businessFieldCode (BUSINESS_FIELD only, UXD-FIN-013) — labels ar/en per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-009/010/016 (e.g. الحقل التجاري لربط الحساب / Account mapping business field · مصدر القيمة / Value source · القيمة الثابتة / Constant value)
Composition       : rule Entry → none (three fields) · Line entry → dimension tags as summary row + second level (count, capped preview "BRANCH ← BRANCH_CODE · ORGANISATION = ORG-1", edit trigger; the tag editor returns the tag list to the line's form state and never saves; RULE-FIN-025 allows at most one tag per dimension) · the constant-account picker (accountDerivationValue) → second level from the Line entry, and the constant dimension-value picker (dimensionValueId) → third level from the tag editor; each picker returns one value and never saves (SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE) · submits: one per sub-view — the rule Entry saves the rule alone, the Line entry saves one line together with its tags; the two never share a surface (ADR-FIN-062)
Permissions       : FIN_RULES — VIEW, CREATE (rule), UPDATE (add line with its tags; deactivate rule — API-FIN-034) — one UPDATE permission covers both (SRS §B4); no DELETE
Cross-module data : eventTypeCode → UXD-FIN-007 · accountDerivationTypeCode → UXD-FIN-008 · accountBusinessFieldCode, tag businessFieldCode → UXD-FIN-013 · valueSourceCode → UXD-FIN-014 · amountSourceTypeCode → UXD-FIN-009 · directionCode → UXD-FIN-002 · distributionTypeCode → UXD-FIN-010
Navigation        : list row → `/fin/rules/:ruleId` · New → `/fin/rules/new`, and after the save the page moves to the record page using the id of the create response · `?lineAction=create` opens the Line entry · a MAPPING line carries a "mappings" link to SCR-FIN-013, filtered by the rule's event type and the line's business field (SRS B1 Navigation) · an id the published search does not return renders a "not found" notice with a way back to the list
States            : empty (no rule; a rule with no line yet) · loading · not found (record page) · error (generic; the one-active-rule-per-event-type rejection surfaces inline on the New form's event type, and the line rejections of RULE-FIN-003, RULE-FIN-018, RULE-FIN-019, RULE-FIN-025 and RULE-FIN-026 surface inline on the Line entry — their catalog codes are Part B's, ADR-FIN-065)

## SCR-FIN-004 — قوالب متكررة/عكسية / Recurring / reversing templates
Traces            : REQ-FIN-022, REQ-FIN-023, REQ-FIN-024, AC-FIN-022
UI pattern        : master-detail RECORD (ADR-FIN-065 — erp-drawer-zustand §11 / ui-master-detail-records): a template OWNS its lines, so the screen is a list page + a record page, not two panes on one route; template update NOT built — open gap (ADR-FIN-006); no by-id endpoint — the record page hydrates on a cold deep link through the published template search filtered by `recurringTemplatePk EQUALS id`, whose response carries the full aggregate (ADR-FIN-065)
Sub-views         : List (templates, whole viewport) · New (header form in a collapsible section + the lines being composed beneath it — the template and its lines are created in ONE call, API-FIN-013, so the lines are composed before the save, not locked) · Record page (header read-only in a collapsible section — no update is published — with its lines beneath, read-only; Run and Deactivate) · Line entry on New (a Drawer on its own search param)
Fields shown      : template: nameAr, nameEn, scheduleTypeCode (UXD-FIN-011), frequencyCode (UXD-FIN-012, required only when scheduleTypeCode=RECURRING), startDate, endDate, isActiveFl · line: accountId, amount, directionCode (UXD-FIN-002), dimensionValueId — per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-011/012
Composition       : line collection → inline beneath the header on the page (it scrolls with the page body, never in a capped region of its own); one line is composed in the Line entry Drawer and returns to the page's line set, never saving · accountId and dimensionValueId → second level (pickers over unbounded sets, SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE — never an inline scrolling option list) · submits: one — the template with its lines in one create call on New; Run and Deactivate are separate confirmed actions on the record page
Permissions       : FIN_RECURRING_TEMPLATES — VIEW, CREATE, UPDATE (run — API-FIN-014; deactivate — API-FIN-036, which since 2026-09-12 also stops the run, `FIN-409-NOT-ACTIVE` — a recorded decision, not a RULE-FIN-*, SRS §B4) — reference only; every affordance renders for a holder of the screen (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065)
Cross-module data : scheduleTypeCode → UXD-FIN-011 · frequencyCode → UXD-FIN-012 · directionCode → UXD-FIN-002
Navigation        : list row → `/fin/recurring-templates/:templateId` · New → `/fin/recurring-templates/new`, and after the save the page moves to the record page using the id of the create response · `?lineAction=create` opens the Line entry on New · an id the published search does not return renders a "not found" notice with a way back to the list
States            : empty · loading · not found (record page) · error (FIN-400-MISSING-FREQUENCY / FIN-404-TEMPLATE / FIN-409-NOT-ACTIVE)

## SCR-FIN-005 — قواعد التوزيع / Allocation rules
Traces            : REQ-FIN-025, REQ-FIN-026, AC-FIN-025
UI pattern        : master-detail RECORD (ADR-FIN-065 — erp-drawer-zustand §11 / ui-master-detail-records): an allocation rule OWNS its targets, so the screen is a list page + a record page, not two panes on one route; rule update NOT built — open gap (ADR-FIN-006); no by-id endpoint — the record page hydrates on a cold deep link through the published allocation-rule search filtered by `allocationRulePk EQUALS id`, whose response carries the targets (ADR-FIN-065)
Sub-views         : List (rules, whole viewport) · New (header form in a collapsible section + the targets being composed beneath it — the rule and its targets are created in ONE call, API-FIN-016, so the targets are composed before the save, not locked) · Record page (header read-only in a collapsible section — no update is published — with its targets beneath, read-only; Run and Deactivate) · Target entry on New (a Drawer on its own search param)
Fields shown      : rule: nameAr, nameEn, sourceAccountId, isActiveFl · target: targetAccountId, dimensionValueId, distributionTypeCode (UXD-FIN-010), distributionValue, isRemainderFl — per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-013/014
Composition       : target collection → inline beneath the header on the page, scrolling with the page body; one target is composed in the Target entry Drawer and returns to the page's target set, never saving · sourceAccountId → second level from the header, targetAccountId and dimensionValueId → pickers from the Target entry (SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE) · submits: one — the rule with its targets in one create call on New; Run and Deactivate are separate confirmed actions on the record page
Permissions       : FIN_ALLOCATION_RULES — VIEW, CREATE, UPDATE (run — API-FIN-017; deactivate — API-FIN-037, stops the run since 2026-09-12, `FIN-409-NOT-ACTIVE` — recorded decision, not a RULE-FIN-*) — reference only; every affordance renders for a holder of the screen (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065)
Cross-module data : distributionTypeCode → UXD-FIN-010
Navigation        : list row → `/fin/allocation-rules/:allocationRuleId` · New → `/fin/allocation-rules/new`, and after the save the page moves to the record page using the id of the create response · `?targetAction=create` opens the Target entry on New · an id the published search does not return renders a "not found" notice with a way back to the list
States            : empty · loading · not found (record page) · error (FIN-404-ALLOCATION-RULE / FIN-422-REMAINDER-MARKER / FIN-422-REMAINDER-NOT-POSITIVE / FIN-409-NOT-ACTIVE)

## SCR-FIN-006 — قيود اليومية / Journal entries (view + manual entry + reverse)
Traces            : REQ-FIN-014, REQ-FIN-016, REQ-FIN-017, REQ-FIN-027, REQ-FIN-028, REQ-FIN-070, REQ-FIN-077, AC-FIN-014, AC-FIN-075, AC-FIN-083
UI pattern        : Search + Entry (with a Reverse action on a POSTED row), ONE screen (composite); by-id read IS built (API-FIN-022) because the entry's lines are needed beyond the search projection (SRS §B1). v2 (SRS SCR-REQ-FIN-006 MODIFIED): the screen itself is unchanged. An event-built entry (journalTypeCode EVENT_GENERATED) is searched, read and reversed like any other, and its lines show their dimension values exactly as a manual line's do (REQ-FIN-070). The event-build operation is a system call with no screen affordance (ADR-FIN-007)
Sub-views         : Search · Entry (manual) · Read (posted, incl. reverse)
Fields shown      : header: docDate, fiscalYearId, periodId, journalTypeCode (UXD-FIN-005, fixed `MANUAL` and read-only on the entry form — ADR-FIN-008), descriptionAr, descriptionEn; system: docNo, statusCode (UXD-FIN-006), postedAt, eventReference, originalEntryId, reversalEntryId · line (repeating): accountId, amount, directionCode (UXD-FIN-002), isRemainderFl (read-only), dimension value(s) — shown as dimension name + value code/name, identical for every journal type, descriptionAr, descriptionEn — per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-004/005/006. `fiscalYearId` is a real input, paired with periodId (RULE-FIN-017 — ADR-FIN-008): the year select lists the fiscal years read through the fiscal-year search (labelled by year code), the period select is narrowed client-side to the periods whose `fiscalYearId` is the chosen year, and the server's year/period/date rejections stay the authority (ADR-FIN-065); both selects disclose when their option set is capped
Composition       : line collection → summary row + second level (line count + running debit/credit totals + edit trigger; the lines Drawer returns the line set to the Entry's form state and never posts) · within it, accountId and each line's dimension value → third-level pickers that return a value (SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE) · Read sub-view → none · submits: one — Post in the Entry's footer (one create call; the balance check stays there); Reverse on a POSTED row is a separate confirmed action of the Read sub-view, never a second save on the Entry
Permissions       : FIN_JOURNAL_ENTRIES — VIEW, CREATE (incl. Post), custom Reverse (`PERM_FIN_JOURNAL_ENTRIES_REVERSE`) — v2 adds no action (the event-build operation is a system call)
Cross-module data : journalTypeCode → UXD-FIN-005 · statusCode → UXD-FIN-006 · directionCode → UXD-FIN-002
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-409-UNBALANCED / FIN-409-NOT-POSTABLE-ACCOUNT / FIN-409-PERIOD-NOT-OPEN / FIN-409-INVALID-DIMENSION / FIN-409-DUPLICATE-EVENT / FIN-400-PERIOD-NOT-IN-YEAR / FIN-400-DOCDATE-OUTSIDE-PERIOD / FIN-404-ENTRY / FIN-409-NOT-POSTED / FIN-409-ALREADY-REVERSED); the not-postable-account and invalid-dimension refusals name no line in the published error body, so they surface once on the lines pane, never on a guessed line (ADR-FIN-065)

## SCR-FIN-007 — الفترات والسنوات المالية / Fiscal periods & years + close approval + year-end close
Traces            : REQ-FIN-031, REQ-FIN-034, REQ-FIN-037, AC-FIN-031
UI pattern        : master-detail RECORD (ADR-FIN-065 — erp-drawer-zustand §11 / ui-master-detail-records): a fiscal year OWNS its periods, so the screen is a list page (years) + a record page (one year and its periods), not two panes on one route; years are read through the published fiscal-year search `POST /api/v1/fin/fiscal-years/search`, which carries no contract id in the api-docs (ADR-FIN-061), periods through the fiscal-period search API-FIN-033 scoped by `fiscalYearId`; no by-id endpoint on either (ADR-FIN-006) — the record page hydrates on a cold deep link through the fiscal-year search filtered by `fiscalYearPk EQUALS id` (ADR-FIN-065)
Sub-views         : List (years, whole viewport) · New (create year — its periods are generated by the create, so the period section is empty with the reason stated until the save) · Record page (the year header read-only in a collapsible section, its periods beneath with their row actions open/soft-close/hard-close, and the year-end close)
Fields shown      : year: code, startDate, endDate, periodCount · period: periodNo, nameAr, nameEn, startDate, endDate, statusCode (UXD-FIN-003), closedBy, closedAt — per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-007/008
Composition       : none — the create-year form has four fields and no secondary detail; the periods are listed inline beneath the year header on the record page; the period transitions are row actions, each one confirmed call · submits: one — Save (create year, which generates its periods)
Permissions       : FIN_PERIODS — VIEW (gateway + the fiscal-period search), CREATE (year), UPDATE (open/soft-close), custom Close-approve (`PERM_FIN_PERIODS_CLOSE_APPROVE` — hard-close, year-end-close; RULE-FIN-015) — reference only; every transition and the year-end close render for a holder of the screen, and the server's FIN-403-FORBIDDEN is the separation-of-duty authority (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065)
Cross-module data : statusCode → UXD-FIN-003 (period) / UXD-FIN-004 (year, binary, not applicable for a lookup select but the code still resolves through the same MDL mechanism)
Navigation        : list row → `/fin/periods/:fiscalYearId` · New → `/fin/periods/new`, and after the save the page moves to the record page using the id of the create response · an id the published search does not return renders a "not found" notice with a way back to the list
States            : empty · loading · not found (record page) · error (FIN-409-YEAR-DUP / FIN-404-YEAR / FIN-404-PERIOD / FIN-409-NOT-REOPENABLE / FIN-409-INVALID-TRANSITION / FIN-409-PERIODS-NOT-CLOSED / FIN-403-FORBIDDEN, shown as the catalogue message on the screen)

## SCR-FIN-008 — دفتر الحساب / Account ledger
Traces            : REQ-FIN-039, REQ-FIN-046, REQ-FIN-071, AC-FIN-039, AC-FIN-076
UI pattern        : FULL_PAGE (no entry sub-view — ADR-FIN-003); single screen (report). v2: event-built and manual lines are listed by the same account-and-dimension filter, whatever their journal type (REQ-FIN-071)
Sub-views         : none
Fields shown      : accountId, accountCode, accountNameAr/En, accountTypeCode (UXD-FIN-001), natureCode (UXD-FIN-002), fromDate/toDate, dimensionId/dimensionValueId, debitTotal, creditTotal, closingBalance, rows[] (journalEntryId, docNo, docDate, journalTypeCode (UXD-FIN-005), eventReference, lineNo, amount, directionCode (UXD-FIN-002), signedAmount, runningBalance, descriptions) — per api-docs `AccountLedgerResponse`
Composition       : none — a read-only report; the filter bar is its only input · submits: none (a filter change re-runs the query)
Permissions       : FIN_ACCOUNT_LEDGER — VIEW
Cross-module data : accountTypeCode → UXD-FIN-001 · natureCode → UXD-FIN-002 · journalTypeCode → UXD-FIN-005 · directionCode → UXD-FIN-002
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-404-ACCOUNT)

## SCR-FIN-009 — ميزان المراجعة / Trial balance
Traces            : REQ-FIN-040, REQ-FIN-046, AC-FIN-040
UI pattern        : FULL_PAGE (no entry sub-view — ADR-FIN-003); single screen (report)
Sub-views         : none
Fields shown      : periodId?, accountTypeCode? (UXD-FIN-001) filters; rows[] (accountId, accountCode, names, accountTypeCode, natureCode (UXD-FIN-002), debitTotal, creditTotal, debitBalance, creditBalance, signedBalance); totalDebitBalance, totalCreditBalance, balanced — per api-docs `TrialBalanceResponse`
Composition       : none — a read-only report · submits: none
Permissions       : FIN_TRIAL_BALANCE — VIEW
Cross-module data : accountTypeCode → UXD-FIN-001 · natureCode → UXD-FIN-002
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-404-PERIOD, only when periodId supplied and unknown)

## SCR-FIN-010 — الميزانية العمومية / Balance sheet
Traces            : REQ-FIN-041, REQ-FIN-046, AC-FIN-041
UI pattern        : FULL_PAGE (no entry sub-view — ADR-FIN-003); single screen (report)
Sub-views         : none
Fields shown      : fiscalYearId (required), asOfDate?; groups[] by accountTypeCode (UXD-FIN-001) → rows[] (accountId, accountCode, names, natureCode (UXD-FIN-002), debitTotal, creditTotal, debitBalance, creditBalance, signedBalance) → groupTotal — per api-docs `BalanceSheetResponse`
Composition       : none — a read-only report · submits: none
Permissions       : FIN_BALANCE_SHEET — VIEW
Cross-module data : accountTypeCode → UXD-FIN-001 · natureCode → UXD-FIN-002
Navigation        : drill-down to the trial balance (SCR-FIN-009) carries the row GROUP's `accountTypeCode` only — the trial balance publishes no account parameter and the balance sheet returns no period, so neither an account nor "the same period" is carried (ADR-FIN-065) · the fiscal-year select discloses when its option set is capped
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-404-YEAR)

## SCR-FIN-011 — قائمة الدخل / Income statement
Traces            : REQ-FIN-042, REQ-FIN-046, AC-FIN-042
UI pattern        : FULL_PAGE (no entry sub-view — ADR-FIN-003); single screen (report)
Sub-views         : none
Fields shown      : fiscalYearId (required), fromPeriodId?, toPeriodId? — each bound independently optional (either, both or neither; an open-ended range is a legal request — ADR-FIN-065); the fiscal-year select discloses when its option set is capped; groups[] by accountTypeCode (UXD-FIN-001, REVENUE/EXPENSE only) → rows[] (as trial balance) → groupTotal; netResult — per api-docs `IncomeStatementResponse`
Composition       : none — a read-only report · submits: none
Permissions       : FIN_INCOME_STATEMENT — VIEW
Cross-module data : accountTypeCode → UXD-FIN-001 · natureCode → UXD-FIN-002
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-404-YEAR / FIN-404-PERIOD)

## SCR-FIN-012 — تقارير الأبعاد / Dimension reports
Traces            : REQ-FIN-043, REQ-FIN-071, AC-FIN-043, AC-FIN-076
UI pattern        : FULL_PAGE (no entry sub-view — ADR-FIN-003); single screen (report). v2: event-built lines now carry dimension values, so they aggregate here with manual lines under the same account-and-value row (REQ-FIN-071); the screen itself is unchanged
Sub-views         : none
Fields shown      : dimensionId (required), dimensionValueId?, periodId?; rows[] (accountId, accountCode, names, natureCode (UXD-FIN-002), dimensionId, dimensionValueId, dimensionValueCode, dimensionValueNameAr/En, debitTotal, creditTotal, signedBalance) — per api-docs `DimensionReportResponse`
Composition       : none — a read-only report · submits: none
Permissions       : FIN_DIMENSION_REPORTS — VIEW
Cross-module data : natureCode → UXD-FIN-002
States            : empty · loading · error (FIN-404-DIMENSION, when the required dimensionId is unknown)

## SCR-FIN-013 — روابط الحسابات / Account mappings
Traces            : REQ-FIN-054, REQ-FIN-055, REQ-FIN-056, REQ-FIN-057, REQ-FIN-058, REQ-FIN-059, REQ-FIN-060, REQ-FIN-061, AC-FIN-058, AC-FIN-059, AC-FIN-060, AC-FIN-061, AC-FIN-062, AC-FIN-063, AC-FIN-064, AC-FIN-065, UXD-FIN-007, UXD-FIN-013, UXD-FIN-015
UI pattern        : Search + Entry, ONE screen (composite) — flat record (SRS SCR-REQ-FIN-013 B1, ADDED v2); server-side search with paging (SRS DEFAULT v2 — page size 20, max 200); the SRS names no by-id read, so the Entry hydrates from the search row (the ADR-FIN-006 pattern), whose published response already carries the mapped account's code and names; its four operations are published (API-FIN-038 search, API-FIN-039 create, API-FIN-040 change account, API-FIN-041 deactivate — ADR-FIN-064)
Sub-views         : Search · Entry (new mapping · change the account of an existing mapping)
Fields shown      : search filters: eventTypeCode (UXD-FIN-007), businessFieldCode (UXD-FIN-013), businessValue (LIKE), accountId, isActiveFl · result columns: eventTypeCode, businessFieldCode, businessValue (a PAYMENT_METHOD value is shown with its label, UXD-FIN-015), account (code + nameAr/nameEn of ENT-FIN-001), isActiveFl · entry: eventTypeCode, businessFieldCode, businessValue, accountId — the first three read-only once saved (ADR-FIN-024); businessValue is a PAYMENT_METHOD select when businessFieldCode=PAYMENT_METHOD (RULE-FIN-024), otherwise the host's code typed as-is, leading and trailing blanks trimmed (ADR-FIN-039); accountId offers active leaf accounts only (RULE-FIN-023) — labels per SRS A3 ENT-FIN-015: نوع الحدث / Event type · الحقل التجاري / Business field · القيمة التجارية / Business value · الحساب / Account · نشط / Active
Composition       : accountId → summary row + second level (the chosen account shows as code + name with a change trigger; the account picker is a second-level Drawer over an unbounded set that returns one account to the Entry's form state and never saves — SECONDARY-DETAIL CLAUSE) · every other field inline (three selects/inputs, no secondary detail; each select discloses when its option set is capped — ADR-FIN-065) · submits: one — Save in the Entry footer (new → create; existing → change account only); Deactivate is a separate confirmed row action, never a second save on the form
Permissions       : FIN_ACCOUNT_MAPPINGS — VIEW (gateway), CREATE (new mapping), UPDATE (change account, and deactivate) — no DELETE, a mapping is deactivated, never hard-deleted (SRS §B4); names follow `PERM_FIN_ACCOUNT_MAPPINGS_<ACTION>`, derived by SEC from the page code — reference only
Cross-module data : eventTypeCode → UXD-FIN-007 · businessFieldCode → UXD-FIN-013 · businessValue (when businessFieldCode=PAYMENT_METHOD) → UXD-FIN-015
Navigation        : from SCR-FIN-003 — a MAPPING line's "mappings" link lands filtered by the rule's event type and the line's business field · to SCR-FIN-001 — the account cell opens the chart of accounts filtered by the mapped account's code (SRS B1 Navigation)
Confirmations     : change-account and deactivate both state REQ-FIN-058's guarantee — ar: "لا يغيّر هذا الإجراء أي قيد سبق ترحيله" · en: "This action does not change any entry already posted"; deactivate adds REQ-FIN-057's consequence — ar: "الأحداث اللاحقة التي تحتاج هذا الربط ستُرفض" · en: "Later events that need this mapping will be rejected"
States            : empty (no mapping matches the filters) · loading · error (generic, catalog code FIN-404-ACCOUNT-MAPPING / FIN-404-ACCOUNT; the rejections of RULE-FIN-022, RULE-FIN-023 and RULE-FIN-024 surface inline on the field they concern — their catalog codes are Part B's)

## Cross-module display dependencies

One `UXD-*` per FIN-owned lookup key that is actually displayed (ADR-FIN-004). The key is the unit
the shared hook is built on, not the screen. Each key resolves through the Lookup module's consumer
read, `API-MDL-011` (`GET /api/v1/mdl/lookups?type=<key>`, `backend-execution-plan-mdl.md`). That
endpoint belongs to a foreign module: it is named here and is never cited as an `API-*` id in
`frontend-execution-plan-fin.md`, because C9.5 binds only FIN's own ids.

v2 (CS-FIN-001) adds three displayed keys:
- **FIN_EVENT_BUSINESS_FIELD** → UXD-FIN-013.
- **FIN_DIMENSION_VALUE_SOURCE** → UXD-FIN-014.
- **PAYMENT_METHOD** → UXD-FIN-015. In v1 it minted no UXD because no FIN field cited it. `ENT-FIN-015.businessValue` now cites it (RULE-FIN-024).

`ACCOUNT_DERIVATION_TYPE` loses DIRECT in MDL (ADR-FIN-010). The screen offers whatever MDL holds, so
UXD-FIN-008 needs no change.

# Frontend execution plan — FIN v2 (خطة تنفيذ الواجهة الأمامية — الحسابات العامة)

Stack : `react-ts-vite` · routing `react-router` · server-state `tanstack-query` · forms
`react-hook-form` · validation `zod` · state `zustand` · one lazy chunk per composite screen
(`profile.stack.frontend`).

Screens (ar/en) : شجرة الحسابات (Chart of accounts) · تعريف الأبعاد وقيمها (Dimensions) ·
قواعد المحرك (Engine rules) · قوالب متكررة/عكسية (Recurring/reversing templates) ·
قواعد التوزيع (Allocation rules) · قيود اليومية (Journal entries) ·
الفترات والسنوات المالية (Fiscal periods & years) · دفتر الحساب (Account ledger) ·
ميزان المراجعة (Trial balance) · الميزانية العمومية (Balance sheet) ·
قائمة الدخل (Income statement) · تقارير الأبعاد (Dimension reports) ·
روابط الحسابات (Account mappings — v2)

Delta v2 (CS-FIN-001): this plan is re-emitted complete. A marker plan folds only its blocks, and the API
surface and alignment content must carry the v2 binding, so every block is written out as current state.
Changes from v1:
- **New screen.** SCR-FIN-013, account mappings, has a SUB in F1 to F4 and a row in SEC-FE.
- **Rewritten screens.** SCR-FIN-003's blocks change for the MAPPING line, the dimension tags and the
  line-per-submit composition (ADR-FIN-062).
- **Bound to the published v2 surface.** The republished api-docs carry CS-FIN-001's endpoints, the v2 members
  of the add-rule-line request and response, and the v2 catalogue codes. Every binding the previous run of this
  stage left pending is now a published id, DTO and code (ADR-FIN-064, which supersedes the BLOCKED ADR-FIN-060).
- **Endpoint citations.** Every endpoint is cited by its published contract id. The one served endpoint the
  api-docs publish without an id, the fiscal-year search, is cited by verb and path (ADR-FIN-061).
- **Composition.** Every F4 block carries its `Composition` and `Saves` lines.
- **Consumer feedback from the frontend build (ADR-FIN-065).**
  - Five screens own a child collection: SCR-FIN-002, 003, 004, 005 and 007. Each becomes a list page, a `/new`
    page and a `/:id` record page. The record page hydrates through its published search filtered by
    `<entity>Pk EQUALS id`, so no by-id endpoint is invented.
  - F1 types every nullable response field as `T | null`.
  - F2 publishes SCR-FIN-006's year options and sends the income-statement bounds independently.
  - Every option read discloses `hasMore`.
  - Two SCR-FIN-003 409s and the journal-entry line refusals are bound.
  - No action affordance is gated client-side.

<!-- PHASE:F1:START traces=SCR-FIN-001,SCR-FIN-002,SCR-FIN-003,SCR-FIN-004,SCR-FIN-005,SCR-FIN-006,SCR-FIN-007,SCR-FIN-008,SCR-FIN-009,SCR-FIN-010,SCR-FIN-011,SCR-FIN-012,SCR-FIN-013 -->
## PHASE F1 — Models & Types

**RF1 — Models & types.** Field/DTO binding: see `_inputs/api-docs-fin.md`. The published request and
response shapes are the source and are not restated here. Every lookup-coded field is typed as `string`
everywhere in the models (ADR-FIN-004), never as an enum or a union of literals, so a value that MDL has not
yet seeded is still assignable to the type. Validity is a runtime concern (§F3), not a type concern. The
same holds in v2, where ACCOUNT_DERIVATION_TYPE loses DIRECT: no type changes, because the option list is
loaded at runtime.

**Nullable response fields (plan-wide, ADR-FIN-065).** Some response fields are `Required: No` in the api-docs,
or are described there as null in some case. Every such field is typed `T | null`, because the wire sends JSON
`null`. It is never typed as optional (`?: T`, which means `T | undefined`). Presence tests on wire data use
`== null` / `!= null`, never `=== undefined` / `!== undefined`. A type that omits `null` hides every wrong branch
from the compiler and from any test that mocks its own fixtures. Request DTOs are unaffected: an absent optional
request member is simply not sent.

Types : `Account` (`AccountResponse`), `AccountCreateRequest`, `AccountUpdateRequest`, `AccountSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-001..004
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-001:END -->

### F1-SCR-FIN-002 — Dimensions
Types : `Dimension` (`DimensionResponse`), `DimensionCreateRequest`, `DimensionValue` (`DimensionValueResponse`), `DimensionValueCreateRequest`, `DimensionSearchRequest`, `DimensionValueSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-005..008; the value deactivate API-FIN-035 returns `DimensionValueResponse`
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-002:END -->

<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-003:START traces=SCR-FIN-003,API-FIN-001,API-FIN-005,API-FIN-008,API-FIN-009,API-FIN-010,API-FIN-011,API-FIN-034,REQ-FIN-007,REQ-FIN-049,REQ-FIN-062,UXD-FIN-013,UXD-FIN-014 -->
### F1-SCR-FIN-003 — Engine rules
Types : `EventTypeRule` (`EventTypeRuleResponse`), `EventTypeRuleCreateRequest`, `RuleLine` (`RuleLineResponse`, embedded in the rule response), `RuleLineCreateRequest`, `EventTypeRuleSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-009..011. The rule deactivate API-FIN-034 returns `EventTypeRuleResponse`. The pickers reuse `Account`, `Dimension` and `DimensionValue` (API-FIN-001, API-FIN-005, API-FIN-008).
v2    : the line's business field is the published `accountBusinessFieldCode` member, and its dimension tags are the published `dimensionTags` array — `RuleLineDimensionTagRequest` on the add-line request, `RuleLineDimensionTagResponse` on every rule and line response (API-FIN-011, and the rule responses of API-FIN-009, API-FIN-010, API-FIN-034). `accountDerivationValue` is optional in the published request, so a MAPPING line leaves it empty as RULE-FIN-019 requires.
Null  : the response members that one derivation or value source leaves unset are `T | null`, never `?: T` (PHASE F1 rule, ADR-FIN-065). That covers `accountDerivationValue` and `accountBusinessFieldCode` on a line, and `dimensionValueId` and `businessFieldCode` on a tag.
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-003:END -->

<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-004:START traces=SCR-FIN-004,API-FIN-012,API-FIN-013,API-FIN-014,API-FIN-036,REQ-FIN-022 -->

Types : `RecurringTemplate` (`RecurringTemplateResponse`, lines included), `RecurringTemplateCreateRequest`, `RecurringTemplateSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-012..014; the deactivate API-FIN-036 returns `RecurringTemplateResponse`
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-004:END -->

<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-005:START traces=SCR-FIN-005,API-FIN-015,API-FIN-016,API-FIN-017,API-FIN-037,REQ-FIN-025 -->
### F1-SCR-FIN-005 — Allocation rules
Types : `AllocationRule` (`AllocationRuleResponse`, targets included), `AllocationRuleCreateRequest`, `AllocationRuleSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-015..017; the deactivate API-FIN-037 returns `AllocationRuleResponse`
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-005:END -->

<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-006:START traces=SCR-FIN-006,API-FIN-018,API-FIN-019,API-FIN-021,API-FIN-022,REQ-FIN-070 -->
### F1-SCR-FIN-006 — Journal entries
Types : `JournalEntry` (`JournalEntryResponse`, lines + line-dimensions included), `JournalEntryCreateRequest`, `JournalEntrySearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-018,019,021,022. `EventEntryBuildRequest`/API-FIN-020 is bound (ADR-FIN-007) but its type is not consumed by any form model — no screen constructs it.
v2    : no type change. An event-built entry's `lines[].dimensions` has the same published shape as a manual entry's (REQ-FIN-070), so one line model serves every journal type.
Null  : `originalEntryId`, `reversalEntryId`, `postedAt` and `eventReference` are `T | null` (PHASE F1 rule, ADR-FIN-065). "Is a reversal", "is reversed" and "can be reversed" test `!= null` / `== null`. An `=== undefined` test on them is always wrong on the wire: it marked every entry a reversal and disabled Reverse for good.
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-006:END -->

<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-007:START traces=SCR-FIN-007,API-FIN-023,API-FIN-024,API-FIN-025,API-FIN-026,API-FIN-027,API-FIN-033,REQ-FIN-031 -->
### F1-SCR-FIN-007 — Fiscal periods & years
Types : `FiscalYear` (`FiscalYearResponse`, periods included on create only), `FiscalYearCreateRequest`, `FiscalYearSearchRequest`, `FiscalPeriod` (`FiscalPeriodResponse`), `FiscalPeriodSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-023..027 and API-FIN-033 (the fiscal-period search); the fiscal-year search is `POST /api/v1/fin/fiscal-years/search` (ADR-FIN-061). A fiscal-year search row always carries an empty `periods` array (api-docs), so the model never reads periods from it.
<!-- SUB:F1-SCR-FIN-007:END -->

### F1-SCR-FIN-013 — Account mappings
Types : `AccountMapping` (`AccountMappingResponse` — it carries the mapped account's code and bilingual name, the display columns of SRS SCR-REQ-FIN-013 B2), `AccountMappingCreateRequest`, `AccountMappingUpdateRequest` (the account alone — ADR-FIN-024), `AccountMappingSearchRequest` — api-docs binding API-FIN-038..041 (search, create, change account, deactivate); the page envelope is the api-docs' `Page<T>`.
Reused: `Account` (`AccountResponse`) and `AccountSearchRequest` — API-FIN-001, for the account picker.
Null  : every response member the api-docs leave not required (the audit fields among them) is `T | null`, never `?: T` (PHASE F1 rule, ADR-FIN-065).

<!-- PHASE:F2:START traces=SCR-FIN-001,SCR-FIN-002,SCR-FIN-003,SCR-FIN-004,SCR-FIN-005,SCR-FIN-006,SCR-FIN-007,SCR-FIN-008,SCR-FIN-009,SCR-FIN-010,SCR-FIN-011,SCR-FIN-012,SCR-FIN-013 -->
## PHASE F2 — Data Hooks

**RF2 — Data hooks.** State rule: page and page size live inside the filter object that forms the cache key.
Components use the facade only, and the facade uses the declared queries only. Every picker (account,
dimension, dimension value) is a read query with its own key. A picker returns a value to its caller's form
state and never writes.

**Option reads (plan-wide, ADR-FIN-065).** Every option read sends page 0 and size 200, the api-docs' maximum
(Pagination Constraints). It publishes `hasMore = totalElements > options.length`. Every consumer of an option
read discloses `hasMore` when the set is capped, and never truncates silently. That covers the picker Drawers
and also every plain `<select>`, such as the year selects of SCR-FIN-006, SCR-FIN-010 and SCR-FIN-011 and the
selects of SCR-FIN-013.

**Record reads (ADR-FIN-065).** SCR-FIN-002, 003, 004, 005 and 007 own a child collection, so each has a
`/:id` record page. Each declares a RECORD-QUERY: the screen's published search, sent with exactly one filter,
`<entity>Pk EQUALS id`, at page 0 and size 1. It sits under the screen's existing query-key root, so every
invalidation this phase already declares for that root refreshes it. A zero-row answer is "not found": an
honest notice with a way back to the list, never an empty form. No by-id endpoint is invented (ADR-FIN-006, as
narrowed). After a create, the record page's id is taken from the CREATE RESPONSE, never re-found in the list.

**Screen gate.** SCREEN-INIT reads the screen's page code from the SEC effective menu (ADR-FIN-005). No
endpoint publishes the caller's action-level permissions. So no hook reads one, and no affordance is gated on
one. FIN-403-FORBIDDEN on a write is mapped to a forbidden rejection, shown as the localized catalogue message
on the screen.

| F2-SCR-FIN-007-FACADE | SCR-FIN-007 | composes YEARS-QUERY, RECORD-QUERY, SEARCH-QUERY + the five mutations · state: year list (YEARS-QUERY, list page), the record page's year (RECORD-QUERY), its periods (SEARCH-QUERY scoped by `fiscalYearId`), filters · every transition and Run-year-end-close render enabled for a holder of the screen; a readiness hint shows while not every period reads HARD_CLOSE, and the server's FIN-409-PERIODS-NOT-CLOSED / FIN-409-NOT-REOPENABLE / FIN-403-FORBIDDEN decide (ADR-FIN-065) |

<!-- PHASE:F3:START traces=SCR-FIN-001,SCR-FIN-002,SCR-FIN-003,SCR-FIN-004,SCR-FIN-005,SCR-FIN-006,SCR-FIN-007,SCR-FIN-008,SCR-FIN-009,SCR-FIN-010,SCR-FIN-011,SCR-FIN-012,SCR-FIN-013 -->
## PHASE F3 — Forms & Validators

No content role in `factory.yaml` names this phase (§3.2), so it is filled per the profile's own convention:
- one `zod` schema per entry or filter form;
- field constraints bound to the published request DTO (`_inputs/api-docs-fin.md`);
- a business rule that a form can usefully pre-empt is named against its `RULE-FIN-*`;
- everything else is left to the server.

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-001:START traces=SCR-FIN-001,REQ-FIN-001,REQ-FIN-002,RULE-FIN-001 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-001 — Chart of accounts
`accountFormSchema` : code (required, ≤30) · nameAr/nameEn (required, ≤200) · accountTypeCode/natureCode (required, member of the runtime-loaded UXD-FIN-001/002 option list) · parentAccountId (optional) · isLeafFl (boolean; disabled and forced false client-side once the account has ≥1 child in the loaded tree — a UX pre-empt of RULE-FIN-001, server remains authoritative)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-001:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-002:START traces=SCR-FIN-002,REQ-FIN-004,REQ-FIN-005,RULE-FIN-002 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-002 — Dimensions
`dimensionFormSchema` : code (required, ≤30) · nameAr/nameEn (required, ≤150)
`dimensionValueFormSchema` : code (required; client-side uniqueness pre-check against the loaded values of the record page's dimension — UX pre-empt of RULE-FIN-002) · nameAr/nameEn (required) · sortOrder (required, integer)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-002:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-003:START traces=SCR-FIN-003,API-FIN-010,API-FIN-011,REQ-FIN-007,REQ-FIN-008,REQ-FIN-009,REQ-FIN-048,REQ-FIN-049,REQ-FIN-062,REQ-FIN-064,REQ-FIN-065,RULE-FIN-003,RULE-FIN-018,RULE-FIN-019,RULE-FIN-025,RULE-FIN-026,UXD-FIN-013,UXD-FIN-014 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-003 — Engine rules
`eventTypeRuleFormSchema` : eventTypeCode (required, ≤50, member of UXD-FIN-007) · nameAr/nameEn (required, ≤150)
`ruleLineFormSchema` — one line together with its tags, one submit (ADR-FIN-062), mapped one to one onto the published `RuleLineCreateRequest` of API-FIN-011:
- **accountDerivationTypeCode.** Required, ≤20, and a member of UXD-FIN-008 as loaded. MDL holds only CONSTANT and MAPPING, so DIRECT is never offered and nothing is hardcoded (RULE-FIN-018).
- **accountDerivationValue.** Required when CONSTANT and empty when MAPPING. It is filled by the account picker, which returns the chosen leaf account's `code` (RULE-FIN-019 pre-empt).
- **accountBusinessFieldCode.** Required when MAPPING and empty when CONSTANT. ≤50, a member of UXD-FIN-013 (RULE-FIN-019 pre-empt).
- **amountSourceTypeCode.** Required, ≤20, a member of UXD-FIN-009.
- **amountSourceValue.** Required unless amountSourceTypeCode=REMAINDER.
- **directionCode.** Required, ≤10, a member of UXD-FIN-002.
- **distributionTypeCode.** Required, ≤15, a member of UXD-FIN-010.
- **isRemainderFl.** Boolean. Cross-field: at most one line of the rule, counting the loaded lines and this one, may carry isRemainderFl=true. That pre-empts a second remainder line under RULE-FIN-003. "Exactly one when any line is PERCENTAGE" stays server-checked, because lines arrive one at a time.
- **dimensionTags.** The list the tag editor returns, sent as the request's `dimensionTags` array.

`dimensionTagFormSchema`, one per tag, mapped onto the published `RuleLineDimensionTagRequest`:
- **dimensionId.** Required, and unique across the line's tags (RULE-FIN-025 pre-empt). The picker offers active dimensions.
- **valueSourceCode.** Required, ≤50, a member of UXD-FIN-014.
- **dimensionValueId.** Required when CONSTANT and empty when BUSINESS_FIELD. The picker is scoped to the tag's dimensionId and to active values (RULE-FIN-026 pre-empt).
- **businessFieldCode.** Required when BUSINESS_FIELD and empty when CONSTANT. ≤50, a member of UXD-FIN-013 (RULE-FIN-026 pre-empt).
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-003:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-004:START traces=SCR-FIN-004,REQ-FIN-022,RULE-FIN-006 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-004 — Recurring/reversing templates
`recurringTemplateFormSchema` : nameAr/nameEn (required, ≤150) · scheduleTypeCode (required, member of UXD-FIN-011) · frequencyCode (required only when scheduleTypeCode=RECURRING, member of UXD-FIN-012 — cross-field, pre-empts FIN-400-MISSING-FREQUENCY) · startDate (required) · endDate (optional)
`recurringTemplateLineFormSchema` : accountId (required) · amount (required, > 0) · directionCode (required, member of UXD-FIN-002) · dimensionValueId (optional) — the set's debit=credit balance (RULE-FIN-006 reused) is checked before submit as a UX pre-empt; the server re-checks at run time
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-004:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-005:START traces=SCR-FIN-005,REQ-FIN-025,RULE-FIN-003 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-005 — Allocation rules
`allocationRuleFormSchema` : nameAr/nameEn (required, ≤150) · sourceAccountId (required)
`allocationTargetFormSchema` : targetAccountId (required) · dimensionValueId (optional) · distributionTypeCode (required, member of UXD-FIN-010) · distributionValue (required unless distributionTypeCode=REMAINDER) · isRemainderFl (boolean) — cross-field across the whole target set before submit: exactly one target carries isRemainderFl=true when any target is PERCENTAGE (RULE-FIN-003; the set is submitted in one create call, so the whole set is known)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-005:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-006:START traces=SCR-FIN-006,REQ-FIN-014,REQ-FIN-017,REQ-FIN-018,RULE-FIN-006,RULE-FIN-017 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-006 — Journal entries
`manualEntryFormSchema` : docDate (required) · fiscalYearId (required; a member of the fiscal-year options the facade publishes — ADR-FIN-065) · periodId (required; option list filtered by the chosen fiscalYearId — UX pre-empt of RULE-FIN-017) · journalTypeCode (fixed `MANUAL`, not a validated input field — ADR-FIN-008) · descriptionAr/descriptionEn (optional) · lines (min 2 — a balanced entry needs at least a debit and a credit side)
`journalLineFormSchema` : accountId (required) · amount (required, > 0) · directionCode (required, member of UXD-FIN-002) · dimensions[] (dimensionId+dimensionValueId pairs, optional) · descriptionAr/descriptionEn (optional) — cross-field: Σdebit = Σcredit checked across `lines` before submit (UX pre-empt of RULE-FIN-006), server remains authoritative
`searchFilterSchema` : docNo (LIKE) · docDate range · periodId · statusCode (member of UXD-FIN-006) · journalTypeCode (member of UXD-FIN-005)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-006:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-007:START traces=SCR-FIN-007,REQ-FIN-031,REQ-FIN-034 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-007 — Fiscal periods & years
`fiscalYearFormSchema` : code (required, ≤10) · startDate/endDate (required, endDate > startDate) · periodCount (required, positive integer)
Row actions (open/soft-close/hard-close/year-end-close) take no form input beyond the row id — the affordance itself is the "form".
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-007:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-008:START traces=SCR-FIN-008,REQ-FIN-039 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-008 — Account ledger
`ledgerFilterSchema` : accountId (required) · fromDate/toDate (optional range) · dimensionId/dimensionValueId (optional pair — dimensionValueId requires dimensionId)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-008:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-009:START traces=SCR-FIN-009,REQ-FIN-040 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-009 — Trial balance
`trialBalanceFilterSchema` : periodId (optional) · accountTypeCode (optional, member of UXD-FIN-001)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-009:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-010:START traces=SCR-FIN-010,REQ-FIN-041 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-010 — Balance sheet
`balanceSheetFilterSchema` : fiscalYearId (required) · asOfDate (optional)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-010:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-011:START traces=SCR-FIN-011,REQ-FIN-042 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-011 — Income statement
`incomeStatementFilterSchema` : fiscalYearId (required) · fromPeriodId (optional) · toPeriodId (optional) — each independently optional, no cross-field rule, as the api-docs publish them (ADR-FIN-065)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-011:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-012:START traces=SCR-FIN-012,REQ-FIN-043 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-012 — Dimension reports
`dimensionReportFilterSchema` : dimensionId (required) · dimensionValueId (optional) · periodId (optional)
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-012:END -->

<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-013:START traces=SCR-FIN-013,API-FIN-038,API-FIN-039,API-FIN-040,REQ-FIN-054,REQ-FIN-055,REQ-FIN-056,REQ-FIN-059,REQ-FIN-060,REQ-FIN-061,RULE-FIN-022,RULE-FIN-023,RULE-FIN-024,UXD-FIN-007,UXD-FIN-013,UXD-FIN-015 -->
### F3-SCR-FIN-013 — Account mappings
`accountMappingFormSchema` (new mapping), bound to the published `AccountMappingCreateRequest` of API-FIN-039:
- **eventTypeCode.** Required, ≤50, a member of UXD-FIN-007.
- **businessFieldCode.** Required, ≤50, a member of UXD-FIN-013.
- **businessValue.** Required, ≤100, with leading and trailing blanks trimmed before submit (ADR-FIN-039); blank after trimming is refused. When businessFieldCode=PAYMENT_METHOD it must be a member of UXD-FIN-015 (RULE-FIN-024 pre-empt). Otherwise it is free text, because the host's code is stored as-is.
- **accountId.** Required, set only through the active-leaf account picker (RULE-FIN-023 pre-empt).

The one-active-mapping-per-key rule, RULE-FIN-022, is not pre-empted client-side, because the loaded page may
not hold the conflicting row. The server's FIN-409-MAPPING-DUP routes inline to the three key fields.

`changeAccountFormSchema` (existing mapping), bound to `AccountMappingUpdateRequest` of API-FIN-040: accountId
only, required and picked as above. The three key fields render read-only and are never submitted (ADR-FIN-024).

`accountMappingFilterSchema` (API-FIN-038): eventTypeCode (member of UXD-FIN-007) · businessFieldCode (member
of UXD-FIN-013) · businessValue (LIKE) · accountId · isActiveFl — all optional, each one a filter field the
published search request supports.
<!-- SUB:F3-SCR-FIN-013:END -->
<!-- PHASE:F3:END -->

<!-- PHASE:F4:START traces=SCR-FIN-001,SCR-FIN-002,SCR-FIN-003,SCR-FIN-004,SCR-FIN-005,SCR-FIN-006,SCR-FIN-007,SCR-FIN-008,SCR-FIN-009,SCR-FIN-010,SCR-FIN-011,SCR-FIN-012,SCR-FIN-013 -->
## PHASE F4 — Screens & Routes

**RF4 — Screens & routes.** Every route element is guarded by its page code being present in the effective
menu that the security module serves (ADR-FIN-005), never by a composed permission string read client-side.
No route is drawn for an operation with no published endpoint (ADR-FIN-006). No action affordance is gated on
the caller's action-level permissions: every affordance renders for a holder of the screen, and FIN-403-FORBIDDEN
is the authority (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065).

**Record pages (ADR-FIN-065, erp-drawer-zustand §11).** SCR-FIN-002, 003, 004, 005 and 007 own a child
collection, and each has three routes:
- a list page;
- `/new`, registered before `/:id`, so that `/new` is never read as an id;
- a `/:id` record page, with the header in a collapsible section and the child collection beneath it.

The child line's add Drawer is on its own search param. After a save, `/new` moves to `/:id`, using the id of
the create response. The record page reads through the screen's RECORD-QUERY, so a cold deep link resolves. An
unresolvable id renders a "not found" notice with a way back.

Every second-level view is its own component and a sibling of the first level. It is opened from
navigation state (a route search param), never from a boolean the screen holds. Its value returns to the
screen's form state.

<!-- SUB:F4-SCR-FIN-001:START traces=SCR-FIN-001,REQ-FIN-001,REQ-FIN-002,REQ-FIN-003,API-FIN-001,API-FIN-002,API-FIN-003,API-FIN-004 -->
### F4-SCR-FIN-001-SCREEN — Chart of accounts
Routes       : `/fin/accounts` (search + inline entry — Composite screen, no `:id` route: entry hydrates from the search row, ADR-FIN-006); filter state in route search params, so a drill-in from SCR-FIN-013 lands filtered by `code`
Guard        : `FIN_ACCOUNTS` present in the effective menu (ADR-FIN-005)
Facade       : F2-SCR-FIN-001-FACADE · page never calls the queries/mutations directly
Cross-module : UXD-FIN-001, UXD-FIN-002 (lookup labels only) — no foreign business data displayed
Composition  : parent-account picker — its own component, a sibling of the Entry, opened from `?picker=parent`, returning the chosen parent to the Entry's form state
Saves        : ONE — create (API-FIN-002) or update (API-FIN-003), a single call each; Deactivate (API-FIN-004) is a confirmed action with its own single call, never on the form
<!-- SUB:F4-SCR-FIN-001:END -->

<!-- SUB:F4-SCR-FIN-002:START traces=SCR-FIN-002,REQ-FIN-004,REQ-FIN-005,REQ-FIN-006,API-FIN-005,API-FIN-006,API-FIN-007,API-FIN-008,API-FIN-035 -->
### F4-SCR-FIN-002-SCREEN — Dimensions
Routes       : `/fin/dimensions` (list) · `/fin/dimensions/new` (header; values locked with the reason stated) · `/fin/dimensions/:dimensionId` (record page: header in a collapsible section, values beneath; hydrated by F2-SCR-FIN-002-RECORD-QUERY — ADR-FIN-065) · navigation state: `?valueAction=create` (Value entry Drawer)
Guard        : `FIN_DIMENSIONS` present in the effective menu
Facade       : F2-SCR-FIN-002-FACADE
Cross-module : none
Composition  : none — neither form has secondary detail; the values collection is inline beneath the header on the record page
Saves        : ONE per form — create dimension (API-FIN-006, the header section's footer), create value (API-FIN-007, the Value entry Drawer); value deactivate (API-FIN-035) is a confirmed row action
<!-- SUB:F4-SCR-FIN-002:END -->

<!-- SUB:F4-SCR-FIN-003:START traces=SCR-FIN-003,REQ-FIN-007,REQ-FIN-008,REQ-FIN-009,REQ-FIN-049,REQ-FIN-062,API-FIN-009,API-FIN-010,API-FIN-011,API-FIN-034,UXD-FIN-002,UXD-FIN-007,UXD-FIN-008,UXD-FIN-009,UXD-FIN-010,UXD-FIN-013,UXD-FIN-014 -->
### F4-SCR-FIN-003-SCREEN — Engine rules
Routes       : `/fin/rules` (list) · `/fin/rules/new` (the rule header, saved alone; lines locked with the reason stated) · `/fin/rules/:ruleId` (record page: rule header read-only in a collapsible section, lines beneath; hydrated by F2-SCR-FIN-003-RECORD-QUERY — ADR-FIN-065; no line-delete affordance — ADR-FIN-006) · navigation state: `?lineAction=create` (Line entry Drawer), `?picker=tags` / `?picker=account` / `?picker=dimension-value` (second and third levels)
Guard        : `FIN_RULES` present in the effective menu; New rule, Add line and Deactivate render for every holder of the screen — FIN-403-FORBIDDEN is the authority (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065)
Facade       : F2-SCR-FIN-003-FACADE
Cross-module : UXD-FIN-007, UXD-FIN-008, UXD-FIN-009, UXD-FIN-002, UXD-FIN-010, UXD-FIN-013, UXD-FIN-014
Composition  : rule Entry — no secondary detail · Line entry — a tags summary row (count + capped preview + edit trigger) whose tag editor is its own component, a sibling of the Line entry, returning the tag list · the constant-account picker (sibling of the Line entry) and the constant dimension-value picker (sibling of the tag editor) each return one value · a MAPPING line row shows a "mappings" link to `/fin/account-mappings?eventTypeCode=…&businessFieldCode=…` (SCR-FIN-013)
Saves        : ONE per sub-view — the rule Entry calls API-FIN-010 alone; the Line entry calls API-FIN-011 once with the line and its `dimensionTags`, whether the line is CONSTANT or MAPPING; the two are never chained (ADR-FIN-062). Deactivate (API-FIN-034) is a confirmed row action with its own single call
<!-- SUB:F4-SCR-FIN-003:END -->

## PHASE SEC-FE — Security (frontend half)

**RF5 — Security.** For each `SCR-*`:
- **Navigation guard.** Without the screen's page code in the SEC effective menu, the user is redirected to
  unauthorized.
- **Action affordances.** No action is gated client-side (ADR-FIN-005, ADR-FIN-065). No published endpoint
  carries the caller's CREATE, UPDATE or custom grants: the SEC effective menu (ADR-FIN-005) carries page codes only. So every affordance
  renders for a caller who holds the screen. A client-side "does this caller hold CREATE" test would have to
  invent the state it reads.
- **Forbidden response.** `FIN-403-FORBIDDEN` is the authority on every write. It is shown as the localized
  catalogue message on the screen, never as an empty result.

Permission names are the backend/SEC registry's own and are never redeclared here (ADR-FIN-005).

| Screen | Navigation guard | Action affordances |
|---|---|---|
| SCR-FIN-001 | `FIN_ACCOUNTS` present → else unauthorized redirect | create, update and deactivate render for every holder of the screen; the server enforces CREATE/UPDATE and answers FIN-403-FORBIDDEN |
| SCR-FIN-002 | `FIN_DIMENSIONS` present (list, `/new` and `/:dimensionId` alike) | create dimension, create value and value-deactivate render for every holder; the server enforces CREATE/UPDATE; dimension itself has no deactivate (SRS §B4) |
| SCR-FIN-003 | `FIN_RULES` present (list, `/new` and `/:ruleId` alike) | new rule, add-line (with its dimension tags) and rule-deactivate render for every holder; the server enforces CREATE and UPDATE — one UPDATE permission covers both (SRS §B4); no line delete |
| SCR-FIN-004 | `FIN_RECURRING_TEMPLATES` present (list, `/new` and `/:templateId` alike) | create, run and deactivate render for every holder; the server enforces CREATE/UPDATE |
| SCR-FIN-005 | `FIN_ALLOCATION_RULES` present (list, `/new` and `/:allocationRuleId` alike) | create, run and deactivate render for every holder; the server enforces CREATE/UPDATE |
| SCR-FIN-006 | `FIN_JOURNAL_ENTRIES` present | create (incl. Post) and Reverse render for every holder; the server enforces CREATE and the custom `PERM_FIN_JOURNAL_ENTRIES_REVERSE`, for event-built entries as for any other |
| SCR-FIN-007 | `FIN_PERIODS` present (list, `/new` and `/:fiscalYearId` alike) | create year, open, soft-close, hard-close and year-end-close all render for every holder, whatever the period's state. RULE-FIN-015's separation of duty is the server's: `FIN-403-FORBIDDEN` answers a caller without Close-approve, and FIN-409-INVALID-TRANSITION / FIN-409-NOT-REOPENABLE / FIN-409-PERIODS-NOT-CLOSED answer on the merits. None of them is duplicated as a client-side check (ADR-FIN-065) |
| SCR-FIN-008 .. SCR-FIN-012 | respective page code present | read-only, VIEW only — no write affordance exists |
| SCR-FIN-013 | `FIN_ACCOUNT_MAPPINGS` present (v2 page, registered by REQ-FIN-044) | new, change-account and deactivate render for every holder; the server enforces the page's CREATE and UPDATE, as the api-docs publish; no Delete affordance exists — a mapping is only ever deactivated (SRS §B4) |

If the menu fails to load or comes back empty, no FIN entry is rendered and no FIN route is granted on any
screen. Access narrows and never widens (ADR-FIN-005).
<!-- PHASE:SEC-FE:END -->
